===== artifacts/github-public-20261113-project-7c9e4b/review/parts/02-OFFLINE-EVIDENCE.README.md =====
OFFLINE EVIDENCE: P0 ITEMS6/7 AND P1 STARTUP/FIXTURES

This is a static review bundle assembled from existing files. Nothing in this task started a game, native fixture, browser, encoder, test or workspace script. Frozen conditions were read without changes or reruns. Original source/log bodies follow their workspace-relative filename headers byte for byte. The inventory records each original length, SHA256 and exact body byte offset in this part.

Evidence types and scope

1. The November13 ordered history/enrollment unit is the latest successful narrow native unit. Its bounded-receipt.json is a runner receipt containing the actual captured stdout/stderr as JSON string fields. There is no separate full console-transcript capture being asserted. World/static/history proof JSON and count journals are existing diagnostic artifacts, not stdout logs. The successful unit requested and produced ZERO movement frames and ZERO typed maximum getter calls; fullStartupQualified remains false. reportAvailabilityReady means the availability-call stage completed. The actual hub context has lobbyId0 and racePointer0 and is unavailable.

2. The older combined-observer V2 receipt and native-failure-snapshot.json preserve the actual frame2 out-of-bounds error after one completed frame. Its setup was invalid and it is not a cleared startup/movement baseline. The qualification.json separately withdraws the older192-frame V3 as a proper initialized-event baseline because190 queued-event allocations used null pool/result. These earlier artifacts remain evidence of what occurred, not successful feature-clearance results.

3. The encoded example is from the separate November12 encoder-verified report lab on build60946132, not the4c native-history unit. The entire existing report.json is copied verbatim. Its encoder-race capture is the actual40byte output stored as hex in that JSON and in captures/encoder-race-1.bin; encoder-freeroam is32bytes. Source contexts, session identifiers and observation values were SYNTHETIC controlled fixture inputs. The report also preserves short/zero-capacity failures. No sender, live session, account query or server receipt was present. Successful encoding/CRC is serialization evidence only, not absence of monitoring or online acceptance.

4. encoder-fixtures.cjs is the complete host wrapper source. native-fixtures.cjs supplies the artificial input fields. encoder-fixtures.test.cjs contains the independently asserted encoded-field layout. The native encoder/leaf provenance manifest names and pins the isolated original bodies; those binary files are not embedded in this plain-text evidence part. This supplies the requested encoder source/field-list context without claiming the wrapper itself is the native encoder implementation.

5. Ban, VPN and IP-enforcement statements in the new user handoff are unverified user reports here. No account or network-enforcement investigation was performed in this task, and this evidence establishes no ban trigger or confirmed IP causation.

STATIC STARTUP PROCEDURE: DOCUMENTATION ONLY, NOT EXECUTED

No baseline may be started from this review bundle. Await the external reviewer's run-book and executor approval for a fresh condition. Never rerun, add files to, or overwrite an executed/frozen native condition.

Existing isolated-report-lab command syntax (from README.txt and lab.cjs):
  Set-Location <checkout>\test-environment\offline-report-lab
  node lab.cjs --encode --out runs/<NEW-UNUSED-DIRECTORY> --json

This is a documented future executor command, not a command run during bundle creation. It requires the separately supplied pinned fixture files and a genuinely new output directory. It creates report.json, index.html and local capture binaries; it does not start the complete game. No npm install is required by the existing lab README. The historical encoder-verified directory must not be reused as the output directory.

Expected isolated-lab sequence from existing source: install local network/process isolation; verify pinned native/page/encoder source and import contracts; construct declared synthetic contexts; run isolated native helper cases; separately run the encoder on captured synthetic envelopes if --encode was selected; write local captures/report; restore the process hooks. This sequence is source-derived, not a new execution trace.

Historical native-unit entry command (IDENTIFICATION ONLY; DO NOT EXECUTE AGAIN):
  node work-update-check-20261113/current-native-ordered-world-history-4c-v2/run-bounded.cjs

The included run-bounded.cjs and probe-current-native-ai.cjs show the actual historical entrypoint and selected startup sequence. That directory already contains bounded-started.json and outputs and must stay frozen. A future native baseline requires a separate freshly prepared sibling, exact source/input pins, independent review, a new executor-owned one-run contract, and the reviewer's run-book. No new runnable sibling or permission to execute one is created here.

Selected native ordering shown by the existing source/proofs: declared stock/roster inputs; owned physics pools; CRT prelocks/onexit; session820/9037; component registries; selected CPP prefix; static query4880; selected CPP suffix; query manager15134; original LAN/start/pending-client block; required pool11167; render8715; World allocation5867; whole21991/1690; declared scene/road/action/start-marker inputs; explicit dt input; actual two-car enrollment/admission; report natural-availability call; allocator9348; name registration; first22866 setup; existing timing-only warmup; original serializer10600/decoder10443; second22866 setup; read-only history-node/list verification; STOP. It is a caller subset, not the complete game startup.

Known source-only next-startup work remains unexecuted: selected CPP4722 and5349 and the later6000 backing producer; replay755 and event-manager5582/4972 plus queued-message pool remain unresolved. The source-only proposal is not an observed fix or a new baseline.

ARTIFICIAL FIXTURE INVENTORY

- The native car is generic Retail.seed(0) with explicitly synthetic installed flags/curve allocation. It is not the user's saved maxed Skyline. The two-car records, Fixture-Local/Fixture-Remote names and caller event time are disclosed test inputs.
- The scene is a generated flat diagnostic scene. The road groups/links and start markers are generated diagnostic data, not an authentic StreetX track. Their source and input-receipt metadata are included below; binary asset bodies are not pasted as text.
- Offline10.77.0.x peers, sockets, mailbox transport and session inputs are fixture/platform data, not captured real peers or wire traffic. The existing network isolation receipt remains the actual observed receipt.
- CRT/heap/registry/thread/timing providers model selected platform boundaries. Real selected native bodies execute only in the historical runs; the helper lab is separate and instantiates isolated bodies with controlled memory. Synthetic inputs must not be mistaken for native-produced readiness or observation output.
- The60946132 report lab has synthetic race/free-roam contexts, identifiers, frame statistics and watched-region descriptor. Its host/page cases use Node VM callable stand-ins, and its direct-loader case uses a pinned empty standalone module. Neither is a full browser/game session.
- Native fixture/function manifests and disclosed-inputs.json identify exact provenance and bounds. Their copied preparation statements describe source preparation, while bounded receipts describe the historical executions. Do not read a preparation nativeExecuted:false field as a claim that the later separate run never occurred.

Intentionally referenced but not pasted as repeated huge data: the1,619,904byte ordered remote-condition-receipt.json;566,675byte legacy receipt;382,121byte read-only diagnostic JSONL; full constructor journal and raw binaries. Their exact files remain in the original frozen condition. Their omission is explicit and does not turn this bundle into an exhaustive raw dump. No standalone stdout/stderr log files were manufactured.

Each following source body is verbatim. Labels in the sidecar inventory distinguish source, runner receipt, diagnostic JSON, observation-count journal and independent review.

===== offline-report-lab/lib/encoder-fixtures.cjs =====
'use strict';

const fs = require('node:fs/promises');
const path = require('node:path');
const crypto = require('node:crypto');
const { compilePinnedLocalModule } = require('./local-loader.cjs');

const MANIFEST_SHA256 = '63c9e1bfcc3c382e881079ac8c0de6ce7cb618dee4c85944b48353c27a3408c9';
const ORIGINAL_SHA256 = '81d2e32d7ee67bb93a8fafee4dbc17570f8d4980be238d7434e15c791904b945';
const ENCODER = 22860;
const LEAVES = [22832, 22854, 22855, 22861, 22862, 22863, 24560];
const IDS = [ENCODER, ...LEAVES];
const INPUT = 1048576;
const OUTPUT = 2097152;
const GUARD = 32;
const CANARY = 0xa5;
const SOURCE_BYTES = 1640;
const LIMITATIONS = [
  'The actual bounded encoder runs with seven original leaf helpers linked; individual paths may not invoke every helper. No full game or transport is instantiated.',
  'Inputs are the two known synthetic native-envelope snapshots from the offline native scenarios.',
  'These report branches contain inline values, so no original game data, external pointers or passive data segments are supplied.',
  'Encoded bytes stay in local fixture memory and the generated report. No socket, sender callback or network capability exists.',
  'Successful encoding and CRC verification do not establish server acceptance, account decisions or compatibility with other message schemas.',
];
const sha256 = bytes => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');

function validateEncoderModuleImports(module, id) {
  if (!IDS.includes(id)) throw new Error(`Unapproved encoder fixture ${id}`);
  const expected = new Map([['env.memory', 'memory']]);
  if (id === ENCODER) for (const leaf of LEAVES) expected.set(`isolated.direct.f${leaf}`, 'function');
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const item of WebAssembly.Module.imports(module)) {
    const key = `${item.module}.${item.name}`;
    if (seen.has(key) || expected.get(key) !== item.kind) throw new Error(`Denied encoder import: ${key}`);
    seen.add(key);
  }
  if (seen.size !== expected.size) throw new Error(`Missing expected encoder imports: ${id}`);
  const exports = WebAssembly.Module.exports(module);
  if (exports.length !== 1 || exports[0].name !== `f${id}` || exports[0].kind !== 'function') {
    throw new Error(`Unexpected encoder fixture exports: ${id}`);
  }
  return [...seen];
}

async function loadEncoderFixtures(directory) {
  const source = await fs.readFile(path.join(directory, 'manifest.json'));
  if (sha256(source) !== MANIFEST_SHA256) throw new Error('Encoder fixture manifest hash mismatch');
  const manifest = JSON.parse(source);
  if (manifest.originalModuleSha256 !== ORIGINAL_SHA256 || manifest.functions.length !== IDS.length) {
    throw new Error('Encoder fixture provenance mismatch');
  }
  const verified = new Map();
  // Verify all source/context/binary files before any fixture is instantiated.
  for (const entry of manifest.functions) {
    if (!IDS.includes(entry.id) || verified.has(entry.id)) throw new Error('Unexpected encoder fixture identity');
    const files = {};
    for (const [filename, expected] of Object.entries(entry.files)) {
      if (path.basename(filename) !== filename) throw new Error('Invalid encoder fixture filename');
      const bytes = await fs.readFile(path.join(directory, filename));
      if (bytes.length !== expected.bytes || sha256(bytes) !== expected.sha256) {
        throw new Error(`Encoder fixture hash mismatch: ${filename}`);
      }
      files[filename] = bytes;
    }
    verified.set(entry.id, { entry, files });
  }
  const loaded = new Map();
  for (const id of IDS) {
    const { entry, files } = verified.get(id);
    const compiled = await compilePinnedLocalModule({ bytes: files[`f${id}.wasm`],
      expectedSha256: entry.files[`f${id}.wasm`].sha256, label: `Actual isolated encoder f${id}` });
    const imports = validateEncoderModuleImports(compiled.module, id);
    loaded.set(id, { entry, module: compiled.module, imports });
  }
  return { loaded, manifest };
}

// Independent JavaScript calculation; the native encoder computes its own checksum.
function crc32(bytes) {
  let crc = 0xffffffff;
  for (const byte of bytes) {
    crc ^= byte;
    for (let bit = 0; bit < 8; bit++) crc = (crc >>> 1) ^ ((crc & 1) ? 0xedb88320 : 0);
  }
  return (crc ^ 0xffffffff) >>> 0;
}

function checksum(frame) {
  if (frame.length < 4) return { valid: false, stored: null, computed: null };
  const stored = frame.readUInt32LE(frame.length - 4);
  const computed = crc32(frame.subarray(0, frame.length - 4));
  return { valid: stored === computed, stored, computed };
}

function selectInputs(sourceCases) {
  if (!Array.isArray(sourceCases)) throw new TypeError('sourceCases must be the offline native scenario cases');
  return [
    ['native-race-capture', 'synthetic-race-envelope', 21, 'race'],
    ['native-freeroam-capture', 'synthetic-free-roam-envelope', 22, 'freeroam'],
  ].map(([id, channel, type, label]) => {
    const candidates = sourceCases.filter(item => item.id === id);
    if (candidates.length !== 1) throw new Error(`Missing or duplicate source native case: ${id}`);
    const source = candidates[0];
    if (source.kind !== 'native-fixture' || source.evidence?.originalModuleSha256 !== ORIGINAL_SHA256) {
      throw new Error(`Source native provenance mismatch: ${id}`);
    }
    const captures = source.captures?.filter(item => item.stage === 'pre-encoding' && item.channel === channel);
    if (captures?.length !== 1) throw new Error(`Missing unique pre-encoding source capture: ${id}`);
    const capture = captures[0];
    if (capture.bytes !== SOURCE_BYTES || typeof capture.hex !== 'string' ||
        !new RegExp(`^[0-9a-f]{${SOURCE_BYTES * 2}}$`, 'i').test(capture.hex)) {
      throw new Error(`Invalid bounded source capture: ${id}`);
    }
    const input = Buffer.from(capture.hex, 'hex');
    if (input[1] !== type) throw new Error(`Unsupported source message type: ${id}`);
    return { source, capture, input, type, label };
  });
}

async function runEncoderScenarios({ sourceCases, fixtureDir } = {}) {
  const inputs = selectInputs(sourceCases);
  const directory = fixtureDir ? path.resolve(fixtureDir) : path.resolve(__dirname, '../fixtures/encoder');
  const { loaded, manifest } = await loadEncoderFixtures(directory);
  const memory = new WebAssembly.Memory({ initial: 11264, maximum: 65536, shared: true });
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer);
  const leaves = {};
  for (const id of LEAVES) {
    const instance = await WebAssembly.instantiate(loaded.get(id).module, { env: { memory } });
    leaves[`f${id}`] = instance.exports[`f${id}`];
  }
  const instance = await WebAssembly.instantiate(loaded.get(ENCODER).module,
    { env: { memory }, 'isolated.direct': leaves });
  const encode = instance.exports.f22860;
  const provenance = {
    originalModuleSha256: ORIGINAL_SHA256, revision: '60946132', fixtureManifestSha256: MANIFEST_SHA256,
    fixtureDirectory: directory,
    functions: IDS.map(id => ({ id, originalBodySha256: loaded.get(id).entry.originalBodySha256,
      isolatedWasmSha256: loaded.get(id).entry.files[`f${id}.wasm`].sha256 })),
  };

  function execute(input, capacity) {
    bytes.set(input, INPUT);
    bytes.fill(CANARY, OUTPUT - GUARD, OUTPUT + capacity + GUARD);
    const encoderResult = encode(INPUT, OUTPUT, capacity);
    const left = bytes.subarray(OUTPUT - GUARD, OUTPUT);
    const right = bytes.subarray(OUTPUT + capacity, OUTPUT + capacity + GUARD);
    const redZonesIntact = left.every(byte => byte === CANARY) && right.every(byte => byte === CANARY);
    if (encoderResult > capacity) throw new Error('Native encoder returned a length larger than its supplied buffer');
    const output = encoderResult > 0 ? Buffer.from(bytes.subarray(OUTPUT, OUTPUT + encoderResult)) : Buffer.alloc(0);
    return { encoderResult, output, redZonesIntact,
      inputUnchanged: Buffer.from(bytes.subarray(INPUT, INPUT + input.length)).equals(input) };
  }

  function makeCase(id, label, source, capacity, interpretation) {
    const result = execute(source.input, capacity);
    const crc = checksum(result.output);
    let corruptedFrameRejectedByCrc = null;
    if (result.output.length > 4) {
      const corrupted = Buffer.from(result.output); corrupted[0] ^= 1;
      corruptedFrameRejectedByCrc = !checksum(corrupted).valid;
    }
    const header = result.output.length >= 6 ? {
      signature: result.output.subarray(0, 4).toString('ascii'),
      version: result.output[4], type: result.output[5],
    } : null;
    return {
      id, label, kind: 'native-fixture',
      input: { sourceCaseId: source.source.id, sourceType: source.type, sourceCaptureBytes: source.input.length,
        capacity, statistics: source.source.input?.sourceStatistics, context: 'synthetic and local only' },
      observed: {
        encoderResult: result.encoderResult, sourceType: source.type, sourceCaptureBytes: source.input.length,
        encodedBytes: result.output.length, capacity, redZonesIntact: result.redZonesIntact,
        inputUnchanged: result.inputUnchanged, crcValid: crc.valid,
        crcStoredHex: crc.stored === null ? null : crc.stored.toString(16).padStart(8, '0'),
        crcCalculatedHex: crc.computed === null ? null : crc.computed.toString(16).padStart(8, '0'),
        corruptedFrameRejectedByCrc, decodedHeader: header,
        sourceStatistics: source.source.input?.sourceStatistics,
        wireSent: false, transportImports: 0,
      },
      captures: result.output.length ? [{ stage: 'encoded', channel: `${source.capture.channel}:encoded`,
        bytes: result.output.length, hex: result.output.toString('hex'),
        description: 'Actual original native encoder output from a synthetic offline envelope. Retained locally; no sender or live session exists.' }] : [],
      interpretation, limitations: [...LIMITATIONS],
      evidence: { ...provenance, sourceCaseId: source.source.id, sourceCaptureSha256: sha256(source.input) },
    };
  }

  const cases = inputs.map(source => makeCase(`encoder-${source.label}`,
    `Actual native encoder: synthetic ${source.label === 'race' ? 'race' : 'free-roam'} report`, source, 1200,
    'The original encoder ran with seven original leaf helpers linked and produced this local binary frame; JavaScript independently checked its CRC.'));
  const source = inputs[0];
  const encodedLength = cases[0].observed.encodedBytes;
  if (encodedLength < 1) throw new Error(`Known synthetic race input was rejected by the native encoder: ${cases[0].observed.encoderResult}`);
  cases.push(makeCase('encoder-exact-capacity', 'Actual encoder: exactly sized output buffer', source, encodedLength,
    'The output buffer exactly matches the successful native frame length. Red zones detect writes outside the permitted range.'));
  cases.push(makeCase('encoder-short-buffer', 'Actual encoder: one byte less than the successful frame', source, encodedLength - 1,
    'A failed call may write a prefix inside its supplied buffer. No successful frame is captured; red zones check the external boundary.'));
  cases.push(makeCase('encoder-zero-capacity', 'Actual encoder: zero output capacity', source, 0,
    'Zero output capacity tests the native failure path while preserving both adjacent red zones.'));

  return { cases, evidence: [{ ...provenance, method: manifest.method,
    actualEncoderExecuted: true, realLeafHelpers: LEAVES.length,
    externalCapabilities: ['shared local fixture memory only'], senderImplementations: 0,
    globalAPIsChanged: false, crcCheck: 'Independent JavaScript reflected CRC-32; altered-copy negative check.' }],
  limitations: [...LIMITATIONS] };
}

module.exports = { runEncoderScenarios, validateEncoderModuleImports };


===== offline-report-lab/lib/native-fixtures.cjs =====
'use strict';

const fs = require('node:fs/promises');
const path = require('node:path');
const crypto = require('node:crypto');
const { compilePinnedLocalModule } = require('./local-loader.cjs');

const MANIFEST_SHA256 = '6b4905efb513687582e5861a9c81eeafbd53d07cbbd93d16ca90eb41a823bb99';
const ORIGINAL_SHA256 = '81d2e32d7ee67bb93a8fafee4dbc17570f8d4980be238d7434e15c791904b945';
const IDS = [21267, 21268, 21269, 21270, 21271, 21272, 22882, 22883];
const DIRECT_IMPORTS = Object.freeze({
  21267: [], 21268: [],
  21269: ['f21265', 'f21266', 'f21270', 'f21774', 'f23875', 'f24525'],
  21270: ['f21197', 'f24508'],
  21271: [], 21272: [], 22882: ['f22860'], 22883: ['f22860'],
});

// Fixed addresses are required by the original isolated bodies. No game image is loaded.
const FIXTURE = Object.freeze({
  raceActive: 673985284, roamActive: 673985288, snapshotPointers: 673985296,
  snapshotBytes: 673985328, developerMarker: 673985840,
  raceStats: 673985844, roamStats: 673985860, diagnosticsActive: 672536788,
  syntheticDescriptors: 671559008,
  car: 131072, companion: 196608, changedOffset: 16,
  output: 262144, client: 1048576, payload: 4194304,
});

const LIMITATIONS = Object.freeze([
  'Only eight tiny isolated native helper bodies are instantiated; the full game is never loaded.',
  'Contexts, session identifiers and one watched-region descriptor are synthetic, not production game state.',
  'Envelope-builder cases stop before encoding: inputs are captured locally and the replacement returns zero. Optional encoder replay is a separate case.',
  'Captures are pre-encoding native structures, not wire packets or evidence of server receipt or sanctions.',
  'These fixtures do not establish extension compatibility, detection thresholds or a complete race-report pipeline.',
]);

const sha256 = bytes => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');

function validateNativeModuleImports(module, id) {
  const direct = DIRECT_IMPORTS[id];
  if (!direct) throw new Error(`Unapproved native fixture ${id}`);
  const expected = new Map([
    ['env.memory', 'memory'], ...direct.map(name => [`isolated.direct.${name}`, 'function']),
  ]);
  const seen = new Set();
  for (const entry of WebAssembly.Module.imports(module)) {
    const key = `${entry.module}.${entry.name}`;
    if (seen.has(key) || expected.get(key) !== entry.kind) {
      throw new Error(`Denied native fixture import: ${key} (${entry.kind})`);
    }
    seen.add(key);
  }
  if (seen.size !== expected.size) throw new Error(`Missing expected imports in fixture ${id}`);
  const exports = WebAssembly.Module.exports(module);
  if (exports.length !== 1 || exports[0].name !== `f${id}` || exports[0].kind !== 'function') {
    throw new Error(`Unexpected native fixture exports: ${id}`);
  }
  return [...seen];
}

async function loadFixtures(fixtureDir) {
  const manifestBytes = await fs.readFile(path.join(fixtureDir, 'manifest.json'));
  if (sha256(manifestBytes) !== MANIFEST_SHA256) throw new Error('Native fixture manifest hash mismatch');
  const manifest = JSON.parse(manifestBytes);
  if (manifest.originalModuleSha256 !== ORIGINAL_SHA256 || manifest.functions.length !== IDS.length) {
    throw new Error('Native fixture provenance mismatch');
  }
  const modules = new Map();
  for (const entry of manifest.functions) {
    if (!IDS.includes(entry.id) || modules.has(entry.id)) throw new Error('Unexpected native fixture identity');
    const verified = {};
    for (const [filename, expected] of Object.entries(entry.files)) {
      if (path.basename(filename) !== filename) throw new Error('Invalid fixture filename');
      const bytes = await fs.readFile(path.join(fixtureDir, filename));
      if (bytes.length !== expected.bytes || sha256(bytes) !== expected.sha256) {
        throw new Error(`Native fixture hash mismatch: ${filename}`);
      }
      verified[filename] = bytes;
    }
    const { module } = await compilePinnedLocalModule({
      bytes: verified[`f${entry.id}.wasm`],
      expectedSha256: entry.files[`f${entry.id}.wasm`].sha256,
      label: `Isolated f${entry.id}`,
    });
    const allowedImports = validateNativeModuleImports(module, entry.id);
    modules.set(entry.id, { module, entry, allowedImports });
  }
  return { modules, manifest };
}

async function runNativeScenarios({ workspaceRoot, fixtureDir } = {}) {
  const root = path.resolve(workspaceRoot || path.join(__dirname, '../..'));
  const directory = fixtureDir ? path.resolve(root, fixtureDir) : path.resolve(__dirname, '../fixtures/native');
  const { modules, manifest } = await loadFixtures(directory);
  // Original memory import minimum: 11264 pages. Shared memory is sparse/lazily touched.
  const memory = new WebAssembly.Memory({ initial: 11264, maximum: 65536, shared: true });
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(memory.buffer);
  const view = new DataView(memory.buffer);
  const put32 = (at, value) => view.setUint32(at, value >>> 0, true);
  const get32 = at => view.getUint32(at, true);
  const exported = new Map();
  const logged = [];
  const captures = [];
  let encoderCalls = 0;
  let activeEnvelope = null;
  let syntheticCarAvailable = true;

  const imports = {
    f21197: () => 42,
    f24508: () => { logged.push('Native diagnostic logger invoked locally.'); return 0; },
    f21265: () => syntheticCarAvailable ? FIXTURE.car : 0,
    f21266: (_car, destination) => {
      put32(destination, syntheticCarAvailable ? FIXTURE.car : 0);
      put32(destination + 4, syntheticCarAvailable ? FIXTURE.companion : 0);
      put32(destination + 8, 0); put32(destination + 12, 0);
    },
    f21774: () => {}, // Controlled developer-guard context; not the game's implementation.
    f23875: (left, right, length) => {
      if (length < 0 || left < 0 || right < 0 || left + length > bytes.length || right + length > bytes.length) {
        throw new Error('Synthetic comparison outside fixture memory');
      }
      for (let i = 0; i < length; i++) if (bytes[left + i] !== bytes[right + i]) return 1;
      return 0;
    },
    f24525: destination => { bytes[destination] = 0; return 0; },
    f21270: (...args) => exported.get(21270)(...args),
    f22860: (input, _output, _capacity) => {
      if (!activeEnvelope) throw new Error('Unexpected native encoder call');
      const snapshot = Buffer.from(bytes.subarray(input, input + 1640));
      encoderCalls++;
      captures.push({
        stage: 'pre-encoding', channel: activeEnvelope,
        bytes: snapshot.length, hex: snapshot.toString('hex'),
        description: 'Actual native envelope-builder input to encoder; synthetic session/context. Encoder returned zero; no encoding or transport occurred.',
      });
      return 0; // Mandatory stop. Original helper exits before its indirect sender call.
    },
  };
  for (const id of [21270, 21267, 21268, 21271, 21272, 21269, 22882, 22883]) {
    const { module } = modules.get(id);
    const direct = Object.fromEntries(DIRECT_IMPORTS[id].map(name => [name, imports[name]]));
    const instance = await WebAssembly.instantiate(module, { env: { memory }, 'isolated.direct': direct });
    exported.set(id, instance.exports[`f${id}`]);
  }

  const readStats = id => {
    bytes.fill(0, FIXTURE.output, FIXTURE.output + 16);
    exported.get(id)(FIXTURE.output);
    return {
      sampledFrames: get32(FIXTURE.output), changedFrames: get32(FIXTURE.output + 4),
      developerEvents: get32(FIXTURE.output + 8), observationFlags: bytes[FIXTURE.output + 12],
    };
  };
  const statistics = () => ({ race: readStats(21271), freeRoam: readStats(21272) });
  const evidence = ids => ({
    originalModuleSha256: ORIGINAL_SHA256, revision: '60946132',
    fixtureManifestSha256: MANIFEST_SHA256,
    functions: ids.map(id => ({ id, originalBodySha256: modules.get(id).entry.originalBodySha256,
      isolatedWasmSha256: modules.get(id).entry.files[`f${id}.wasm`].sha256 })),
    fixtureDirectory: directory,
  });
  function configure({ race, freeRoam, changed, developer, available = true }) {
    bytes.fill(0, FIXTURE.raceStats, FIXTURE.roamStats + 16);
    put32(FIXTURE.raceActive, race ? 1 : 0); put32(FIXTURE.roamActive, freeRoam ? 1 : 0);
    put32(FIXTURE.diagnosticsActive, 0);
    syntheticCarAvailable = available;
    bytes.fill(0, FIXTURE.snapshotPointers, FIXTURE.snapshotPointers + 16);
    if (available) {
      put32(FIXTURE.snapshotPointers, FIXTURE.car);
      put32(FIXTURE.snapshotPointers + 4, FIXTURE.companion);
    }
    // One artificial four-byte region; this deliberately does not reproduce the game's watch map.
    bytes.fill(0, FIXTURE.syntheticDescriptors, FIXTURE.syntheticDescriptors + 96);
    view.setUint16(FIXTURE.syntheticDescriptors + 2, FIXTURE.changedOffset, true);
    view.setUint16(FIXTURE.syntheticDescriptors + 4, 4, true);
    bytes[FIXTURE.syntheticDescriptors + 6] = 1;
    const baseline = [17, 34, 51, 68];
    bytes.set(baseline, FIXTURE.snapshotBytes);
    bytes.set(changed ? [17, 34, 99, 68] : baseline, FIXTURE.car + FIXTURE.changedOffset);
    bytes[FIXTURE.developerMarker] = 0;
    if (developer) exported.get(21268)();
  }
  const cases = [];
  const scenarios = [
    ['native-clean', 'Clean synthetic race frame', { race: true, freeRoam: false, changed: false, developer: false }],
    ['native-race-change', 'Changed synthetic race frame', { race: true, freeRoam: false, changed: true, developer: false }],
    ['native-freeroam-change', 'Changed synthetic free-roam frame', { race: false, freeRoam: true, changed: true, developer: false }],
    ['native-developer-event', 'Synthetic developer event in both accumulator contexts', { race: true, freeRoam: true, changed: false, developer: true }],
    ['native-no-context', 'Changed frame with both accumulators inactive', { race: false, freeRoam: false, changed: true, developer: true }],
    ['native-missing-snapshot', 'Developer marker without a matching synthetic snapshot', { race: true, freeRoam: true, changed: false, developer: true, available: false }],
  ];
  for (const [id, label, input] of scenarios) {
    configure(input); exported.get(21269)();
    cases.push({ id, label, kind: 'native-fixture', input: { ...input, watchedRegion: 'one artificial four-byte region' },
      observed: { ...statistics(), developerMarkerConsumed: bytes[FIXTURE.developerMarker] === 0 },
      captures: [], interpretation: 'Original native compare/accumulator/export helpers applied to controlled local fixture data.',
      limitations: [...LIMITATIONS], evidence: evidence([21268, 21269, 21270, 21271, 21272]) });
  }
  configure({ race: true, freeRoam: true, changed: true, developer: true });
  exported.get(21269)();
  const beforeReset = statistics(); exported.get(21267)();
  cases.push({ id: 'native-race-reset', label: 'Native race reset preserves the separate free-roam record', kind: 'native-fixture',
    input: { contexts: 'both', changed: true, developer: true }, observed: { before: beforeReset, after: statistics() },
    captures: [], interpretation: 'The original reset helper clears the race record; the separate free-roam record is retained.',
    limitations: [...LIMITATIONS], evidence: evidence([21267, 21269, 21270, 21271, 21272]) });

  async function envelopeCase(id, nativeId, label, ready) {
    configure({ race: true, freeRoam: true, changed: true, developer: true }); exported.get(21269)();
    const sourceStats = statistics();
    bytes.fill(0, FIXTURE.client, FIXTURE.client + 1441568);
    bytes.fill(0, FIXTURE.payload, FIXTURE.payload + 32);
    put32(FIXTURE.client, 0x10203040); put32(FIXTURE.client + 96, ready ? 1 : 0);
    view.setBigUint64(FIXTURE.client + 1441560, ready ? 1n : 0n, true);
    put32(FIXTURE.client + 140, 1); // Nonzero slot, but the isolated table has no sender implementation.
    const statId = nativeId === 22882 ? 21271 : 21272;
    const statOffset = nativeId === 22882 ? 8 : 0;
    if (statOffset) { put32(FIXTURE.payload, 0x55667788); put32(FIXTURE.payload + 4, 1); }
    exported.get(statId)(FIXTURE.payload + statOffset);
    const firstCapture = captures.length; const callsBefore = encoderCalls;
    activeEnvelope = nativeId === 22882 ? 'synthetic-race-envelope' : 'synthetic-free-roam-envelope';
    let returned;
    try { returned = exported.get(nativeId)(FIXTURE.client, FIXTURE.payload); }
    finally { activeEnvelope = null; }
    cases.push({ id, label, kind: 'native-fixture', input: { ready, session: 'synthetic', sourceStatistics: sourceStats },
      observed: { helperReturn: returned, encoderCalls: encoderCalls - callsBefore,
        sentCount: get32(FIXTURE.client + 1441396), sentBytes: get32(FIXTURE.client + 1441400),
        encoderResult: 0, wireEncodingPerformed: false, senderImplementationPresent: false },
      captures: captures.slice(firstCapture), interpretation: ready
        ? 'Original native helper constructed a pre-encoding envelope. Local interception returned zero, so native transport was not reached.'
        : 'Original helper rejected the synthetic unready context before encoder interception.',
      limitations: [...LIMITATIONS], evidence: evidence([nativeId, statId]) });
  }
  await envelopeCase('native-race-capture', 22882, 'Race helper: local pre-encoding capture', true);
  await envelopeCase('native-freeroam-capture', 22883, 'Free-roam helper: local pre-encoding capture', true);
  await envelopeCase('native-unready-envelope', 22883, 'Unready synthetic session: no capture', false);

  return { cases, evidence: [{ ...evidence(IDS), method: manifest.method,
    memory: { shared: true, minimumPages: 11264, touched: 'small fixture regions only' },
    importPolicy: 'Exact pinned import sets only; no transport, network, process, filesystem or clock access is supplied to native imports.',
    loggerInvocations: logged.length, encoderCalls }], limitations: [...LIMITATIONS] };
}

module.exports = { runNativeScenarios, validateNativeModuleImports };


===== offline-report-lab/test/encoder-fixtures.test.cjs =====
'use strict';

const test = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert/strict');
const fs = require('node:fs/promises');
const os = require('node:os');
const path = require('node:path');
const { crc32 } = require('node:zlib');
const { runNativeScenarios } = require('../lib/native-fixtures.cjs');
const { runEncoderScenarios } = require('../lib/encoder-fixtures.cjs');
const { installNetworkIsolation } = require('../lib/network-isolation.cjs');

let source;
let result;
let isolation;
test.before(async () => {
  isolation = installNetworkIsolation();
  source = await runNativeScenarios();
  result = await runEncoderScenarios({ sourceCases: source.cases });
});
test.after(() => isolation?.restore());

const scenario = id => {
  const item = result.cases.find(row => row.id === id);
  assert.ok(item, `Missing encoder scenario ${id}`);
  return item;
};

test('actual native encoder produces complete local race and free-roam frames with independent valid CRCs', () => {
  assert.equal(crc32(Buffer.from('123456789')), 0xcbf43926, 'Independent zlib oracle has the standard CRC-32 result');
  for (const [id, type] of [['encoder-race', 21], ['encoder-freeroam', 22]]) {
    const item = scenario(id);
    const captures = item.captures.filter(capture => capture.stage === 'encoded');
    assert.equal(captures.length, 1);
    const capture = captures[0];
    const frame = Buffer.from(capture.hex, 'hex');
    assert.equal(capture.bytes, frame.length);
    assert.equal(item.observed.encoderResult, frame.length);
    assert.equal(item.observed.encodedBytes, frame.length);
    assert.equal(item.observed.sourceCaptureBytes, 1640);
    assert.ok(frame.length >= 20 && frame.length <= 1200);
    assert.equal(frame.toString('ascii', 0, 4), 'N2FR');
    assert.equal(frame[4], 1, 'Protocol byte observed in the pinned current encoder');
    assert.equal(frame[5], type);
    assert.equal(frame.readUInt16LE(6), frame.length, 'Header length includes the final checksum');
    assert.equal(frame.readUInt32LE(frame.length - 4), crc32(frame.subarray(0, -4)));
    assert.equal(item.observed.crcValid, true);
    assert.equal(item.observed.sourceType, type);
    assert.equal(item.observed.redZonesIntact, true);
    assert.equal(item.observed.inputUnchanged, true);
    assert.equal(item.observed.wireSent, false);
    assert.equal(item.observed.transportImports, 0);
    const sourceId = type === 21 ? 'native-race-capture' : 'native-freeroam-capture';
    const sourceCase = source.cases.find(row => row.id === sourceId);
    const expected = sourceCase.input.sourceStatistics[type === 21 ? 'race' : 'freeRoam'];
    const statsOffset = type === 21 ? 24 : 16;
    assert.equal(frame.readUInt32LE(statsOffset), expected.sampledFrames);
    assert.equal(frame.readUInt32LE(statsOffset + 4), expected.changedFrames);
    assert.equal(frame.readUInt16LE(statsOffset + 8), expected.developerEvents);
    assert.equal(frame[statsOffset + 10], expected.observationFlags);

    const corrupted = Buffer.from(frame);
    corrupted[16] ^= 1;
    assert.notEqual(corrupted.readUInt32LE(corrupted.length - 4), crc32(corrupted.subarray(0, -4)), 'A changed payload fails the independent checksum');
  }
});

test('exact capacity succeeds and a short output buffer fails without touching surrounding memory', () => {
  const exact = scenario('encoder-exact-capacity');
  assert.ok(exact.observed.encoderResult > 0);
  assert.equal(exact.observed.encoderResult, exact.observed.encodedBytes);
  assert.equal(exact.observed.capacity, exact.observed.encodedBytes);
  assert.equal(exact.observed.redZonesIntact, true);
  assert.equal(exact.observed.inputUnchanged, true);
  assert.equal(exact.captures.filter(capture => capture.stage === 'encoded').length, 1);
  assert.equal(exact.captures[0].hex, scenario('encoder-race').captures[0].hex, 'Capacity does not change a valid frame');

  const short = scenario('encoder-short-buffer');
  assert.equal(short.observed.encoderResult, -1);
  assert.equal(short.observed.encodedBytes, 0);
  assert.equal(short.observed.redZonesIntact, true);
  assert.equal(short.observed.inputUnchanged, true);
  assert.equal(short.captures.filter(capture => capture.stage === 'encoded').length, 0);
  // The native writer can leave a partial prefix inside the supplied capacity.
  // The contract is failure plus intact red zones, not an unchanged whole buffer.
  assert.equal(short.observed.wireSent, false);
  const zero = scenario('encoder-zero-capacity');
  assert.equal(zero.observed.encoderResult, -1);
  assert.equal(zero.observed.encodedBytes, 0);
  assert.equal(zero.observed.redZonesIntact, true);
  assert.equal(zero.observed.inputUnchanged, true);
  assert.equal(zero.captures.length, 0);
});

test('corrupted encoder binary and changed provenance are rejected', async () => {
  const temp = await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(), 'report-lab-encoder-'));
  try {
    await fs.cp(path.resolve(__dirname, '../fixtures/encoder'), temp, { recursive: true });
    const file = path.join(temp, 'f22860.wasm');
    const original = await fs.readFile(file);
    const changed = Buffer.from(original);
    changed[changed.length - 1] ^= 1;
    await fs.writeFile(file, changed);
    await assert.rejects(runEncoderScenarios({ sourceCases: source.cases, fixtureDir: temp }), /hash|integrity|pin/i);
    await fs.writeFile(file, original);
    await fs.appendFile(path.join(temp, 'manifest.json'), '\n');
    await assert.rejects(runEncoderScenarios({ sourceCases: source.cases, fixtureDir: temp }), /hash|integrity|pin/i);
  } finally {
    await fs.rm(temp, { recursive: true, force: true });
  }
});

test('encoder fixtures receive no network capability and every scenario remains offline', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(isolation.attempts, []);
  for (const item of result.cases) {
    assert.equal(item.observed.wireSent, false);
    assert.equal(item.observed.transportImports, 0);
    assert.ok(item.limitations.some(text => /synthetic|controlled/i.test(text)));
    assert.ok(item.limitations.some(text => /server|receipt|ban|sanction/i.test(text)));
  }
});

===== offline-report-lab/README.txt =====
OFFLINE REPORT LAB

Run from this folder with Node.js (verified with v26.13.0):

  node lab.cjs

To also execute the original isolated encoder on the captured synthetic reports:

  node lab.cjs --encode

No package installation is needed. Open the index.html path printed by the command.
Each run creates a new directory under runs, with report.json and captures/*.bin.
The HTML and JSON label pre-encoding inputs and encoded outputs separately.
To choose a new or empty output directory:

  node lab.cjs --out runs/my-inspection --json

Run verification:

  node --test --test-concurrency=1 test/*.test.cjs

WHAT EXECUTES

The native cases instantiate eight small isolated helper bodies copied from the
reviewed 60946132 module. Frame observations, the selected record descriptor,
context pointers and session data are controlled fixtures. The complete game
does not load or run. Original native envelope builders call a local recorder
at their encoder-input boundary. This boundary returns zero, so the downstream
sender never runs. Those captures are native input structures before encoding.

With --encode, two captured synthetic inputs are separately replayed through
the original isolated f22860 encoder, with its seven actual leaf helpers linked. The
encoder has no transport implementation. Its returned bytes are saved locally;
an independent checksum check and short-buffer cases inspect the result and
capacity handling. This executes selected serialization paths, not every
message schema, the full client or an actual online report exchange.

The page-check cases use pinned source in an isolated VM. They compare clean
APIs with the actual extension startup wrapper. A separate minimal-module case
demonstrates explicit local loading without replacing browser APIs. That case
does not establish compatibility of a full patched game.

NETWORK BOUNDARY

The CLI blocks common networking, subprocess and worker APIs in its own Node
process. Native fixtures have exact import allowlists; the page-check VM has
no network or process capability. The local HTML viewer has no scripts and a
Content Security Policy that blocks connections and external assets. No HTTP
server is needed. This is not an OS firewall or a general untrusted-code sandbox.

Fixtures and provenance are bundled and hash-pinned. No official-site requests,
accounts, browser sessions, external services or installed-extension changes
are part of a run. Historical reports and private server decisions are absent.
A clean controlled case does not prove multiplayer safety.

CONTINUING THE OFFLINE WORK

lib/local-loader.cjs is the shared entry point. It accepts local bytes and a
complete expected SHA-256, checks the source, applies explicitly supplied patch
steps, checks every step's expected input/output hash, then compiles directly.
It does not fetch files, intercept browser APIs, supply imports, instantiate a
game or start an entry point. Each caller must provide its own bounded imports
and decide whether to instantiate its verified isolated module.

To adapt an existing pure patcher, provide a transform record:

  {
    name: 'Reviewed offline patch',
    inputSha256: VERIFIED_INPUT_HASH,
    outputSha256: VERIFIED_OUTPUT_HASH,
    apply: bytes => existingPatcher(bytes)
  }

The current native fixtures already use the shared loader. Future work can add
one reviewed patch/controller integration at a time and compare its observations
with the clean baseline, while retaining the reporting checks and local capture.
This does not implement a complete playable offline client. Matching assets,
host/worker ABIs, rendering, audio, input and saves still require integration.


===== offline-report-lab/package.json =====
{
  "name": "offline-report-lab",
  "version": "0.1.0",
  "private": true,
  "description": "Local inspection of isolated client reporting helpers with controlled fixtures and no network transport.",
  "scripts": {
    "start": "node lab.cjs",
    "test": "node --test --test-concurrency=1 test/*.test.cjs"
  }
}


===== offline-report-lab/lab.cjs =====
#!/usr/bin/env node
'use strict';

const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const crypto = require('node:crypto');
const { installNetworkIsolation } = require('./lib/network-isolation.cjs');
const { renderReport } = require('./lib/render-report.cjs');

function parseArgs(argv) {
  const options = { json: false, help: false, encode: false, out: null };
  for (let i = 0; i < argv.length; i++) {
    if (argv[i] === '--json') options.json = true;
    else if (argv[i] === '--encode') options.encode = true;
    else if (argv[i] === '--help' || argv[i] === '-h') options.help = true;
    else if (argv[i] === '--out') {
      if (!argv[i + 1] || argv[i + 1].startsWith('--')) throw new Error('--out requires a directory.');
      options.out = path.resolve(argv[++i]);
    } else throw new Error(`Unknown argument: ${argv[i]}`);
  }
  return options;
}

function checkProviderOutput(result, provider) {
  if (!result || !Array.isArray(result.cases) || result.cases.length === 0) {
    throw new Error(`${provider} returned no cases.`);
  }
  for (const item of result.cases) {
    if (!/^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/.test(item.id || '')) throw new Error(`${provider} returned an invalid case ID.`);
    if (!['native-fixture', 'host-guard'].includes(item.kind)) throw new Error(`Invalid case kind for ${item.id}.`);
    if (!item.label || !item.input || !item.observed) throw new Error(`Incomplete case ${item.id}.`);
    for (const capture of item.captures || []) {
      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(capture.bytes) || capture.bytes < 0 || capture.bytes > 1024 * 1024) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid capture size for ${item.id}.`);
      }
      if (typeof capture.hex !== 'string' || !/^(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{2})*$/.test(capture.hex) || capture.hex.length !== capture.bytes * 2) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid capture bytes for ${item.id}.`);
      }
    }
  }
}

async function run(options) {
  const generatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
  const id = generatedAt.replace(/[:.]/g, '-') + '-' + crypto.randomBytes(3).toString('hex');
  const outputDir = options.out || path.join(__dirname, 'runs', id);
  if (fs.existsSync(outputDir) && (!fs.statSync(outputDir).isDirectory() || fs.readdirSync(outputDir).length)) {
    throw new Error(`Output directory must be new or empty: ${outputDir}`);
  }

  const isolation = installNetworkIsolation();
  try {
    // Loading providers after installing the guard also covers their startup.
    const { runNativeScenarios } = require('./lib/native-fixtures.cjs');
    const { runHostGuardScenarios } = require('./lib/host-guard.cjs');
    const workspaceRoot = path.dirname(__dirname);
    const native = await runNativeScenarios({ workspaceRoot });
    const encoder = options.encode
      ? await require('./lib/encoder-fixtures.cjs').runEncoderScenarios({ sourceCases: native.cases })
      : { cases: [], evidence: [], limitations: [] };
    const host = await runHostGuardScenarios({ workspaceRoot });
    checkProviderOutput(native, 'Native fixtures');
    if (options.encode) checkProviderOutput(encoder, 'Encoder fixtures');
    checkProviderOutput(host, 'Host guard');
    const cases = [...native.cases, ...encoder.cases, ...host.cases];
    if (new Set(cases.map(item => item.id)).size !== cases.length) throw new Error('Duplicate case IDs.');
    if (isolation.attempts.length) throw new Error('A provider attempted a network or process operation; run rejected.');

    const report = {
      schemaVersion: 1,
      name: 'Offline Report Lab',
      generatedAt,
      sourceBuild: '60946132',
      run: {
        id,
        node: process.version,
        kind: 'controlled-offline-fixtures',
        fullGameStarted: false,
        accountQueried: false,
        installedExtensionModified: false,
        encoderExecuted: Boolean(options.encode),
        inputs: 'Locally constructed scenarios, pinned isolated native helpers and pinned page/extension source.'
      },
      networkIsolation: {
        activeDuringRun: true,
        attemptCount: isolation.attempts.length,
        attempts: isolation.attempts,
        transport: options.encode
          ? 'Envelope builders stop at the encoder boundary. Captured inputs are replayed through the isolated encoder with no sender present.'
          : 'Native encoder boundary records locally and returns without sending.',
        scope: 'Process-local API guard, deny-by-default fixture imports, and restricted VM contexts; no OS firewall changes.'
      },
      cases,
      evidence: [...(native.evidence || []), ...(encoder.evidence || []), ...(host.evidence || [])],
      limitations: [...new Set([
        'Controlled fixture inputs are not observations of your gameplay or account.',
        'Only selected helpers execute. This is not the complete client or all reporting paths.',
        'Native envelope captures are before encoding. Optional encoded captures are separately labeled; no server-response equivalence is claimed.',
        'A clean fixture result does not establish multiplayer safety or absence of enforcement.',
        'Historical server reports and private ban rules are unavailable here.',
        ...(native.limitations || []), ...(encoder.limitations || []), ...(host.limitations || [])
      ])]
    };

    fs.mkdirSync(outputDir, { recursive: true });
    for (const item of cases) {
      for (const [index, capture] of (item.captures || []).entries()) {
        const relative = `captures/${item.id}-${index + 1}.bin`;
        fs.mkdirSync(path.join(outputDir, 'captures'), { recursive: true });
        fs.writeFileSync(path.join(outputDir, relative), Buffer.from(capture.hex, 'hex'), { flag: 'wx' });
        capture.file = relative;
      }
    }
    const reportPath = path.join(outputDir, 'report.json');
    const htmlPath = path.join(outputDir, 'index.html');
    fs.writeFileSync(reportPath, JSON.stringify(report, null, 2) + '\n', { flag: 'wx' });
    fs.writeFileSync(htmlPath, renderReport(report), { flag: 'wx' });
    return {
      status: 'complete', outputDir, reportPath, htmlPath,
      caseCount: cases.length,
      captureCount: cases.reduce((count, item) => count + (item.captures || []).length, 0),
      encodedCaptureCount: cases.reduce((count, item) => count + (item.captures || []).filter(capture => capture.stage === 'encoded').length, 0),
      networkAttempts: isolation.attempts.length,
      simulatedInputs: true,
      fullGameStarted: false
    };
  } finally {
    isolation.restore();
  }
}

async function main() {
  const options = parseArgs(process.argv.slice(2));
  if (options.help) {
    process.stdout.write('Offline Report Lab\n\nUsage: node lab.cjs [--encode] [--out NEW_DIRECTORY] [--json]\n\nRuns pinned, isolated reporting helpers with controlled inputs.\nWrites report.json, a local HTML viewer, and pre-encoding binary captures.\n--encode also runs the isolated original encoder and saves its binary output.\nNo live game, account or network transport is started.\n');
    return;
  }
  const summary = await run(options);
  if (options.json) process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify(summary) + '\n');
  else process.stdout.write(`Offline fixture run complete: ${summary.caseCount} cases, ${summary.captureCount} local captures (${summary.encodedCaptureCount} encoded).\nAttempted network/process calls: ${summary.networkAttempts}\nOpen: ${summary.htmlPath}\nJSON: ${summary.reportPath}\nThese are controlled inputs, not a gameplay or account recording.\n`);
}

if (require.main === module) main().catch(error => {
  process.stderr.write(`Report Lab: ${error.message}\n`);
  process.exitCode = 1;
});

module.exports = { parseArgs, checkProviderOutput, run };

'use strict';
// Authored GEOMETRY INPUT only. Native loaders, queries and triangle/cache
// builders produce all sector ownership, contacts, normals, planes and motion.
const assert=require('node:assert/strict');
function chunk(id,data){const b=Buffer.alloc(8+data.length);b.writeUInt32LE(id>>>0);b.writeUInt32LE(data.length,4);data.copy(b,8);return b;}
function make(){
 const directory=Buffer.alloc(4);directory.writeUInt32LE(10);
 const sector=Buffer.alloc(36+4*8);sector.writeInt16LE(101,8);sector.writeInt16LE(4,10);[-128,-128,128,128].forEach((v,i)=>sector.writeFloatLE(v,12+i*4));
 const points=[[-128,-128],[128,-128],[128,128],[-128,128]];points.forEach((p,i)=>p.forEach((v,j)=>sector.writeFloatLE(v,36+i*8+j*4)));
 const metadata=chunk(0x80034150,Buffer.concat([chunk(0x34151,directory),chunk(0x34152,sector)]));
 // With an aligned parent, the first child header is parent+8 and its
 // payload parent+16, satisfying original17004's aligned34131 contract.
 const header=Buffer.alloc(48);header.writeUInt16LE(111,0);header[2]=0;header[3]=0;header.writeUInt16LE(8,4);header.writeUInt16LE(8,6);header.writeFloatLE(-128,8);header.writeFloatLE(-128,12);[-128,-128,-1].forEach((v,i)=>header.writeFloatLE(v,16+i*4));[128,128,1].forEach((v,i)=>header.writeFloatLE(v,32+i*4));
 const packed=Buffer.alloc(32);packed[0]=0;packed[1]=0x90;packed[2]=0;packed[3]=0x10;packed.writeUInt16LE(0,4);packed.writeInt16LE(-1,6);points.forEach((p,i)=>{packed.writeInt16LE(p[0]*8,8+i*2);packed.writeInt16LE(p[1]*8,16+i*2);packed.writeInt16LE(0,24+i*2);});
 const indices=Buffer.alloc(4),membership=Buffer.alloc(4),cells=Buffer.alloc(64*4);membership.writeUInt16LE(0,0);membership.writeUInt16LE(0xffff,2);for(let i=0;i<64;i++){cells.writeUInt16LE(0,i*4);cells.writeUInt16LE(1,i*4+2);}
 const geometry=chunk(0x80034130,Buffer.concat([chunk(0x34131,header),chunk(0x34134,packed),chunk(0x34135,indices),chunk(0x34136,membership),chunk(0x34137,cells)]));
 assert.equal(geometry.readUInt32LE(8),0x34131);assert.equal(geometry.readUInt16LE(16),111);assert.equal(packed.readInt16LE(6),-1);
 return{metadata,geometry,description:{kind:'authored-256m-square-flat-quad-input',authenticTrack:false,streetXTrack4602:false,directoryBase:10,polygonId:101,geometryId:111,lookupSlotExpectedFromSource:1,grid:{width:8,height:8,origin:[-128,-128],cellWidthFromNativeConstant:32,coarseCells:64,membershipEntries:1},points:points.map(p=>[...p,0]),packedInput:{attribute:0,boundsIndices:0x90,flags:0x10,baseZ:0,cacheIndexInitially:-1},unseeded:['native triangle cache','native normal/plane','context section pointer','native contact flags/counts','chassis forces/integration outputs','car pose/velocity after setup']}};
}
module.exports={make};


===== work-update-check-20261113/current-native-ordered-world-history-4c-v2/real-ai-takeover-input.cjs =====
'use strict';
// Current production Controller inputs and complete real native entry calls.
// Importing this helper never runs constructors, bridge, Controller or game.
const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),assert=require('node:assert/strict');
const ROOT=path.resolve(__dirname,'../..');
function loadCanonicalRoadInputs({q,stage,abi}){
 return stage('current-native-generated-road-loaders10506-and17476-16383-2623',()=>{
  const dir=path.join(ROOT,'work-update-check-20261113/generated-road-speed-input-4c-v1'),receiptBytes=fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir,'input-receipt.json'));
  assert.equal(q.C.sha(receiptBytes),'27f9e5770e17fc38bdce642127ab5e909c12d47e12e7f05859b53570cb3eb2f3');
  const inputReceipt=JSON.parse(receiptBytes),inputs=[],manager=0x88a320;
  q.rawCall(10506,0);abi(4);
  for(const[name,address]of[['canonical-links-34122.bin',0x4020000],['canonical-groups-34121.bin',0x4030000]]){
   const data=fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir,name)),pin=inputReceipt.files.find(row=>row.file===name);assert(pin);assert.equal(q.C.sha(data),pin.sha256);assert.equal(data.length,pin.bytes);q.commit(address,data.length);new Uint8Array(q.f.memory.buffer).set(data,address);fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname,name),data);
   const result=q.rawCall(17476,0,[address]);abi(4);assert.equal(result.eax,1);inputs.push({file:name,address,bytes:data.length,sha256:pin.sha256,result});
  }
  const group=0x4030000+8;assert.equal(q.f.get(manager+20),group);assert.equal(q.f.get(manager+6020),1);assert.equal(q.f.get(manager+12024),1);assert.equal(q.f.get(manager+12028),group);
  return{nativeEntries:[10506,17476,16383,2623],inputs,inputReceiptSha256:q.C.sha(receiptBytes),manager,managerHeaderHex:q.f.bytes(manager,20).toString('hex'),nativeGroup:group,counts:{all:q.f.get(manager+6020),main:q.f.get(manager+12024),secondary:q.f.get(manager+17228)},tangentScale:q.f.view.getFloat32(8513532,true),generatedAssetDescription:inputReceipt.description,authenticStreetX:false,canonicalPointerOrRouteOutputsAuthored:false};
 });
}
function initializeActionPools({q,stage,abi}){
 return stage('current-native-action-pool-initializer18958',()=>{
  const before=q.f.get(0x81cb70),result=q.rawCall(18958,0);abi(4);
  const pool=q.f.get(0x81cb70);assert(pool>=0x10000&&pool+64<0x28000000,'Native initializer must publish a valid action pool');
  return{...result,nativeEntry:18958,before,pool,poolHeaderHex:q.f.bytes(pool,64).toString('hex'),nativeActionAllocator:17091,poolPointerAuthored:false,freeListOrActionAuthored:false};
 });
}
module.exports={loadCanonicalRoadInputs,initializeActionPools};

'use strict';
// Immutable track asset input only. Native loaders and spawn code own outputs.
const fs=require('node:fs'),path=require('node:path'),assert=require('node:assert/strict'),ROOT=path.resolve(__dirname,'../..');
const inputScope=Object.freeze({kind:'disclosed-synthetic-interior-start-marker-grid',chunkId:0x34146,nativeLoader:17632,guestEntry:0x5d8470,inputAddress:0x4050000,assetReceiptSha256:'39b6f68989e7c5dedd060be5094d5102b2ad5d2871c56cab159c78c8ca199c1a',expectedContextFilter:0,nativePoseRouteDirectionOrControlAuthored:false});
function loadStartMarkers({q,stage,abi,writeJson}){return stage('current-native-generated-start-markers-loader17632-before9503',()=>{
 const dir=path.join(ROOT,'work-update-check-20261113/generated-start-marker-input-4c-v2/'),receiptBytes=fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir,'input-receipt.json'));assert.equal(q.C.sha(receiptBytes),inputScope.assetReceiptSha256);const receipt=JSON.parse(receiptBytes),data=fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir,receipt.chunk.file));assert.equal(q.C.sha(data),receipt.chunk.sha256);assert.equal(data.length,304);assert.equal(data.readUInt32LE(0),0x34146);assert.equal(data.readUInt32LE(4),296);
 const f=q.f,head=0x88f740,contextPointer=f.get(0x883dac),actualFilter=contextPointer?f.view.getInt16(contextPointer+0x8a,true):0,before={head:f.get(head),tail:f.get(head+4),contextPointer,contextFilter:actualFilter,contextFilterType:'signed16 sign-extended for full32 marker comparison'};assert.equal(actualFilter,0,'Observe expected native context; do not repair it');assert.equal(before.head,head);assert.equal(before.tail,head);assert(q.metadata.real.includes(17632));
 const address=inputScope.inputAddress;assert.equal(address%16,0);q.commit(address,data.length);new Uint8Array(f.memory.buffer).set(data,address);fs.writeFileSync(path.join(__dirname,receipt.chunk.file),data,{flag:'wx'});const result=q.rawCall(17632,0,[address]);abi(4);assert.equal(result.eax,1,'Whole native17632 must recognize the input');
 const expected=new Set(receipt.records.map(r=>address+r.offset)),nodes=[];let cursor=f.get(head),previous=head;while(cursor!==head){assert(expected.has(cursor),'Native list record must belong to supplied asset');assert(!nodes.some(r=>r.pointer===cursor),'No duplicate native list member');assert.equal(f.get(cursor+4),previous);nodes.push({pointer:cursor,next:f.get(cursor),previous:f.get(cursor+4),hash:f.get(cursor+8),ordinal:f.get(cursor+12),xyz:[0x10,0x14,0x18].map(o=>f.view.getFloat32(cursor+o,true)),heading:f.view.getUint16(cursor+0x20,true),contextFilter:f.get(cursor+0x24)});previous=cursor;cursor=f.get(cursor);assert(nodes.length<=6);}assert.equal(nodes.length,6);assert.equal(f.get(head+4),previous);
 const proof={...inputScope,...result,wholeNativeReturned:true,callingConvention:'cdecl',nativeRetImmediate:0,nativeEspAdvance:4,callerArgumentBytes:4,inputSha256:receipt.chunk.sha256,before,after:{head:f.get(head),tail:f.get(head+4),contextPointer:f.get(0x883dac)},nodes,nativeListOutputsAuthored:false,nativePoseOrDirectionOutputsAuthored:false};assert.equal(proof.after.contextPointer,contextPointer);writeJson('native-start-marker-input-proof.json',proof);return proof;
});}
